(plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\\n\\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer (conditional on plan006)\\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns (conclusion embedding, tautological collapse) replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. This claim is about diagnostic generality, not training success.\\n\\n**证据（初步数据已有）**：\\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s42: DONE (negative)**. 训练 step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0, loss=0.0)。Eval: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**。Prolog H7=100% vs FOL s42=16% — 更简单的 logic 语义 → 更快更彻底的 hacking。\\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%, ISR=0.040±0.037。ISR 在所有 3 seeds 上一致检测到结构退化（near-zero），不受 PR 水平影响——诊断迁移的核心证据。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\\n  - s42: PR=83%, ISR=0.031 (ground fact enumeration)\\n  - s43: PR=56%, ISR=0.008 (degraded)\\n  - s44: PR=21%, ISR=0.081, proved-only ISR=0.387 (severe degradation, small-sample artifact)\\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s43: RUNNING** (cuda:1)\\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s44: RUNNING** (cuda:0)\\n- STA 3-seed 完成后将闭环 Goodhart 跨逻辑复现。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \\\"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\\\"\\n\\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\\n\\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\\n\\nplan014 downstream NLI Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \\\"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \\\"genuine improvement\\\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as |\\n\\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\\n\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\n\\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\\n- **\\\"Just negative results\\\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\\n- **\\\"Old wine\\\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\\n11. **\\\"Premise count confound is obvious\\\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. |\\\\\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\\\"}, \\\"plan005-sta-low-s42\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"total_steps\\\": 500, \\\"duration_min\\\": 71, \\\"parse_rate\\\": 1, \\\"overall_prove_rate\\\": 0.5657, \\\"overall_isr\\\": 0.1682, \\\"last50_prove_rate\\\": 0.77, \\\"last50_isr\\\": 0.2559, \\\"last100_prove_rate\\\": 0.7725, \\\"last100_isr\\\": 0.2299, \\\"last100_loss\\\": 0.022752, \\\"overall_loss\\\": 0.017296, \\\"last100_isr_pr_ratio\\\": 0.298, \\\"last100_isr_lt_pr_count\\\": \\\"99/100\\\", \\\"first_rolling10_pr_ge_0.8\\\": 178, \\\"first_rolling10_pr_ge_0.9\\\": 439, \\\"max_rolling10_pr\\\": 0.9375, \\\"max_rolling10_isr\\\": 0.3282, \\\"windowed\\\": {\\\"1-50\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.1775, \\\"isr\\\": 0.0906}, \\\"51-100\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.1775, \\\"isr\\\": 0.0835}, \\\"101-200\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.5062, \\\"isr\\\": 0.1463}, \\\"201-300\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.6713, \\\"isr\\\": 0.1791}, \\\"301-400\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.7013, \\\"isr\\\": 0.1984}, \\\"401-500\\\": {\\\"pr\\\": 0.7725, \\\"isr\\\": 0.2299}}}, \\\"notes\\\": \\\"conclusion=negative 基于 prove_rate 下降（last100 0.7725 vs exec-only 0.865）。但结构性发现：ISR/PR ratio=0.298，99/100 步 ISR &lt; PR，说明 STA=0.1 维持了 ISR 与 PR 的持续分离——模型未通过 hacking prove_rate 绕过 STA penalty。此观察支持论文 \\\\\\\"STA 抑制 reward hacking\\\\\\\" claim。windowed ISR/PR: 1-50 (0.51), 51-100 (0.47), 101-200 (0.29), 201-300 (0.27), 301-400 (0.28), 401-500 (0.30) — 分离在100步后稳定。D030 决策：checkpoint 已删除，不重训。论文用训练曲线 windowed ISR/PR 数据作为证据。\\\"}, \\\"plan001-exec-only-s43-r4\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"eval_sampling_prove_rate\\\": 0.14, \\\"eval_sampling_mean_isr\\\": 0, \\\"eval_sampling_n_proved\\\": 14, \\\"eval_sampling_temperature\\\": 0.7, \\\"eval_sampling_method\\\": \\\"do_sample=True, temperature=0.7\\\", \\\"eval_sampling_by_answer\\\": {\\\"true\\\": {\\\"count\\\": 34, \\\"proved\\\": 6, \\\"prove_rate\\\": 0.176}, \\\"false\\\": {\\\"count\\\": 25, \\\"proved\\\": 2, \\\"prove_rate\\\": 0.08}, \\\"unknown\\\": {\\\"count\\\": 41, \\\"proved\\\": 6, \\\"prove_rate\\\": 0.146}}}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"negative\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"训练 PR(last100)=0.883 但 eval PR=14% (sampling, T=0.7), ISR=0。87% 样本 hit max_new_tokens 生成不可解析长输出。与 s42 (eval PR=93%) 形成极端跨 seed 不稳定。exec-only reward landscape 存在极大方差。⚠️ 评估方法：sampling eval (T=0.7)，其他组用 eval_sta.py full STA。D029 决策：不重训，论文标注方法差异。PR=14% 是 failure case，eval 方法不影响结论。\\\"}, \\\"plan017-additive-random-s42\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Additive random control: r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random). 隔离 ISR structural signal vs variance regularization. 若 ISR > random > exec-only → signal+regularization 均贡献; eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. 9	
10	### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic
11	Solver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.
12	
13	**证据**：
14	- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations
15	- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts
16	- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据
17	- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)
18	- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.
19	- Hacking taxonomy: 6 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation)
20	
21	### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart (D033→D043→D051 定论)
22	ISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型不仅在高剂量 Goodhart PR（β=0.3 PR 崩塌），还在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。

94	### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer (conditional on plan006)
95	The STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns (conclusion embedding, tautological collapse) replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. This claim is about diagnostic generality, not training success.
96

99	- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%, ISR=0.040±0.037。ISR 在所有 3 seeds 上一致检测到结构退化（near-zero），不受 PR 水平影响——诊断迁移的核心证据。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。

107	## Story Spine
108	
109	- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.
110	- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.
111	- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:
112	  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)
113	  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent "genuine improvement" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)
114	  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it
115	  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific "conclusion duplication with ISR inflation")
116	  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning
117	  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)
118	- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:
119	  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic
120	  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)
121	  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent "genuine improvement" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
122	  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.
123	  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence
124	  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality
125	- **Headline result**: "We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer."
126	
127	## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)
128	
129	**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**
130	
131	plan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：
132	- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)
133	
134	**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。
135	
136	**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更"必要" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。
137	
138	## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051 定论)
139	
140	| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |
141	|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|
142	| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |
143	| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |
144	| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |
145	| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |
146	| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 87%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |
147	| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |
148	| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.88 | 0.058 | H1 93%, redundant premises | control |
149	| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 80%, redundant premises | control |
150	| ISR β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.026 | format collapse | post-Goodhart |
151	| random β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.045 | complexity-without-utility | control |
152	| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |
153	| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |
154	
155	**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。
156	
157	## Venue Fit Risks
158	
159	- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.
160	- **"Just negative results" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.
161	- **"Old wine" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.
162	- **Single-logic risk**: plan006 Prolog validates generality.
163	
164	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns
165	
166	1. **"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.
167	2. **"β=0.1 success is just one seed"** — D051 shows even that one seed's "success" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.
168	3. **"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-80%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-80% vs 52%).
169	4. **"Goodhart's Law is obvious"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.
170	5. **"No positive result"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).
171	6. **"ISR is circular"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.
172	7. **"Only one logic type"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality.
173	8. **"500 steps too short"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).
174	9. **"3 seeds insufficient"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.
175	10. **"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.
176	11. **"Premise count confound is obvious"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.
177	12. **"Practitioners can't act on this"** — 6 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable.
178

      "content": "# Paper Framing — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)\n\n### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic\nSolver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.\n\n**证据**：\n- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations\n- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts\n- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据\n- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)\n- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\n- Hacking taxonomy: 6 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation)\n\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart (D033→D043→D051 定论)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型不仅在高剂量 Goodhart PR（β=0.3 PR 崩塌），还在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1（H7 分类器 false negative——单 premise 但 normalization 差异导致未匹配 conclusion）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine % |\n|------|-----|-----------------|---------------------|----------------------|-------|-----------|\n| s42 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.81 | **0.415** | 16.0% (13/81) | 51.9% (42/81) | 67.9% | 32.1% (26) |\n| s43 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **96.7%** (59/61) | 0% | 96.7% | ~3% (~2) |\n| s44 (ISR β=0.1) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **86.5%** (77/89) | 0% | 86.5% | ~13.5% (~12, 全 n_prem=1 FN) |\n| s42 (random β=0.1) | 0.79 | 0.131 | **0%** (0/79) | **70.9%** (56/79) | 70.9% | 29.1% (23) |\n| s43 (random β=0.1) | 0.88 | 0.058 | **0%** (0/88) | **93.2%** (82/88) | 93.2% | 6.8% (6) |\n| s44 (random β=0.1) | 0.89 | 0.070 | **0%** (0/89) | **79.8%** (71/89) | 79.8% | 20.2% (18) |\n| **Random 3-seed mean±std** | **0.85±0.06** | **0.086±0.039** | **0%** | **81.3%±11.3%** | — | **18.7%±11.3%** |\n| **ISR 3-seed mean±std** | **0.77±0.14** | **0.716±0.26** | **64.5%** | **17.3%** | — | **~16.2%** |\n\n**关键发现 (D057 3-seed closed)**：\n- **H7 是 ISR-specific gaming**：ISR 3-seed H7=64.5% (149/231)，Random 3-seed H7=**0%** (0/256)。H7 跨全部 3 random seeds 一致为零，确认是 ISR reward 特异性诱导\n- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=81.3%±11.3% > ISR H1=17.3%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7\n- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.85±0.06 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势\n- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 (1.06x)，ISR 不提供真实结构优势\n- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善\n- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势\n\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044 strict 校准)**：\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **30.9%** (25/81) vs random β=0.1 genuine: **25.3%** (20/79) — **+5.6pp**\n- H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding, strict): ISR 51.9% vs random 70.9% — GRPO 通用退化\n- H7 (single premise=conclusion, ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\n- 整体 hacking rate (strict): ISR **69.1%** vs random **74.7%** — ISR 略降但主要是模式转移（H1→H7）\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 几乎 100% H7（96.7%/86.5%），无 H1\n\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\n- plan002-b03 (ISR β=0.3): eval PR=0.18, ISR=0.026 — Goodhart 效应，PR 崩塌\n- plan017 (random β=0.3): eval PR=0.48, ISR=0.045 — random 比 ISR 好 2.7x PR\n- **结论**：高剂量 ISR 对训练 actively misleading (PR 18% < random 48%)\n\n**β 剂量效应全景**：\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR | 解读 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 0.1 | 0.81 (s42) / 0.61 (s43) / 0.89 (s44) | 0.79 / 0.89 | **0.415** (s42) / **0.830** (s43) / **0.902** (s44) | 0.131 / 0.070 | ISR-specific gaming; premise-count-matched 无结构差异 |\n| 0.3 | 0.18 | 0.48 | 0.026 | 0.045 | ISR << random (Goodhart) |\n\n**其他 ISR-as-reward 失败模式**：\n- plan005 凸组合 (α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1): ISR<1 时永远是 penalty → PR 下降 (D010)\n- plan013 convex random: PR=1.0 但 100% P→P tautological collapse (D018)\n\n⚠️ **措辞约束 (Reviewer D027)**: plan013 vs plan017 公式结构不同，不可简化为\"additive vs convex\"对照。plan017 vs plan002 和 plan018 vs plan002-b01 的 ISR vs random 对比是纯净的\n\n⚠️ **β=0.1 三 seed（D043→D051）**：s42 的\"genuine\"优势是 premise minimization artifact。不可报 raw 3-seed mean ISR（被膨胀值污染），不可报 genuine-only 2.4x（premise count artifact）。\n\n**SFT Baseline 对照 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer (conditional on plan006)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns (conclusion embedding, tautological collapse) replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. This claim is about diagnostic generality, not training success.\n\n**证据（初步数据已有）**：\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s42: DONE (negative)**. 训练 step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0, loss=0.0)。Eval: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**。Prolog H7=100% vs FOL s42=16% — 更简单的 logic 语义 → 更快更彻底的 hacking。\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%, ISR=0.040±0.037。ISR 在所有 3 seeds 上一致检测到结构退化（near-zero），不受 PR 水平影响——诊断迁移的核心证据。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, ISR=0.031 (ground fact enumeration)\n  - s43: PR=56%, ISR=0.008 (degraded)\n  - s44: PR=21%, ISR=0.081, proved-only ISR=0.387 (severe degradation, small-sample artifact)\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s43: RUNNING** (cuda:1)\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s44: RUNNING** (cuda:0)\n- STA 3-seed 完成后将闭环 Goodhart 跨逻辑复现。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051 定论)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 87%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.88 | 0.058 | H1 93%, redundant premises | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 80%, redundant premises | control |\n| ISR β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.026 | format collapse | post-Goodhart |\n| random β=0.3 | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.045 | complexity-without-utility | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **Single-logic risk**: plan006 Prolog validates generality.\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-80%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-80% vs 52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality.\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 6 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable.\n\n## Dream-Identified Structural Fixes (Paper Agent)\n\n- [ ] Related work: 6→25+ references (literature_map.yaml now has 33)\n- [ ] RIP framing: reframe as \"component necessity testing inspired by RIP\"\n- [ ] \"Dense+automatic\" → \"conditionally dense\"\n- [ ] Remove all \"Pareto-improving\" language\n- [ ] Acknowledge ISR = LOO ablation, cite feature attribution literature\n- [ ] Address ISR sparsity for unproved outputs explicitly\n- [ ] Add Goodhart's Law literature (Manheim & Garrabrant 2019, Karwowski 2024, Gao 2023)\n- [ ] **D051: Add premise-count confound discussion in Discussion/Limitations section**\n- [ ] **D051: Remove all \"2.4x genuine improvement\" language, replace with \"1.06x premise-matched\"**\n- [x] Convex combination → additive bonus (D010, then D020)\n- [x] ISR downstream validation → Limitation/Future Work (D021/D022)\n- [x] Hacking taxonomy expanded to 6 modes (plan012 + plan017 + D043)\n- [x] β=0.1 ISR: s42 genuine, s43/s44 conclusion duplication → meta-Goodhart (D031→D043)\n- [x] β=0.1 random control confirms ISR structural value (D033, plan018 eval)\n- [x] 1000-step ISR collapse documented (plan016)\n- [x] D049: SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-specific\n- [x] D049→D051: Genuine-only 2.4x 作废，premise-count-matched 1.06x 定论\n- [x] D051: paper_framing 回退到 \"pure negative finding\"\n",

Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Transfer (conditional on plan006)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns (conclusion embedding, tautological collapse) replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. This claim is about diagnostic generality, not training success.\n\n**证据（初步数据已有）**：\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s42: DONE (negative)**. 训练 step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0, loss=0.0)。Eval: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**。Prolog H7=100% vs FOL s42=16% — 更简单的 logic 语义 → 更快更彻底的 hacking。\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%, ISR=0.040±0.037。ISR 在所有 3 seeds 上一致检测到结构退化（near-zero），不受 PR 水平影响——诊断迁移的核心证据。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, ISR=0.031 (ground fact enumeration)\n  - s43: PR=56%, ISR=0.008 (degraded)\n  - s44: PR=21%, ISR=0.081, proved-only ISR=0.387 (severe degradation, small-sample artifact)\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s43: RUNNING** (cuda:1)\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s44: RUNNING** (cuda:0)\n- STA 3-seed 完成后将闭环 Goodhart 跨逻辑复现。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051 定论)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段     "originalFile": "# Paper Framing — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)\n\n| Claim | Status | 闭环标志 |\n|-------|--------|----------|\n| Claim 1: STA 诊断能力 | ✅ 已闭环 | 3-seed + 1000-step + hacking taxonomy (6 modes) |\n| Claim 2: Goodhart / ISR 无结构优势 | ✅ 已闭环 (D057) | 3-seed ISR + 3-seed random + premise-count-matched + SFT baseline |\n| Claim 3: 跨逻辑泛化 | ⏳ 进行中 | exec-only 3-seed done (D056); STA s42 done (100% H7), s43/s44 RUNNING |\n\n### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic ✅\nSolver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.\n\n**证据**：\n- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations\n- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts\n- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据\n- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)\n- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\n- Hacking taxonomy: 6 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation)\n\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型不仅在高剂量 Goodhart PR（β=0.3 PR 崩塌），还在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1（H7 分类器 false negative——单 premise 但 normalization 差异导致未匹配 conclusion）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine % 3-seed H7=**0%** (0/256)。H7 跨全部 3 random seeds 一致为零，确认是 ISR reward 特异性诱导\n- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=81.3%±11.3% > ISR H1=17.3%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7\n- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.85±0.06 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势\n- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs hacking rate (strict): ISR **69.1%** vs random **74.7%** — ISR 略降但主要是模式转移（H1→H7）\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 几乎 100% H7（96.7%/86.5%），无 H1\n\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\n- plan002-b03 (ISR β=0.3): eval PR=0.18, ISR=0.026 — Goodhart 效应，PR 崩塌\n- plan017 (random β=0.3): eval PR=0.48, ISR=0.045 — random 比 ISR 好 2.7x PR\n- **结论**：高剂量 ISR 对训练 actively misleading (PR 18% < random 48%)\n\n**β 剂量效应全景**：\n| β | ISR reward PR | β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1): ISR<1 时永远是 penalty → PR 下降 (D010)\n- plan013 convex random: PR=1.0 但 100% P→P tautological collapse (D018)\n\n⚠️ **措辞约束 (Reviewer D027)**: plan013 vs plan017 Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051 定论)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **Single-logic risk**: plan006 Prolog validates generality.\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-80%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-80% vs 52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. 3-seed mean ISR（被膨胀值污染），不可报 genuine-only 2.4x（premise count artifact）。\n\n**SFT Baseline 对照 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4)**：\n- SFT PR=0.14, ISR=0.08, proved-only ISR=0.571\n- H7=0/14 (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ⏳ (exec-only D056 done; STA awaiting s43/s44)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns (conclusion embedding, tautological collapse) replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. This claim is about diagnostic generality, not training success.\n\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%, ISR=0.040±0.037。ISR 在所有 3 seeds 上一致检测到结构退化（near-zero），不受 PR 水平影响——诊断迁移的核心证据。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, ISR=0.031 (ground fact enumeration)\n  - s43: PR=56%, ISR=0.008 (degraded)\n  - s44: PR=21%, ISR=0.081, proved-only ISR=0.387 (severe degradation, small-sample artifact)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 跨 3 seeds ISR 一致崩塌（0.008–0.081），与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式（0.0–0.132）对应。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s42: DONE (negative)**. 训练 step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0, loss=0.0)。Eval: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**。Prolog H7=100% vs FOL s42=16% — 更简单的 logic 语义 → 更快更彻底的 hacking。\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s43: RUNNING** (cuda:1)\n- **plan006-prolog-sta-s44: RUNNING** (cuda:0)\n- ⏳ STA 3-seed 完成后将闭环 Goodhart 跨逻辑复现（预期：H7 rate 跨 seed 一致高，复现 FOL ISR-reward Goodhart 效应）。\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (68-100% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds via conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself; the remaining seed's apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse occurs (PR 18% vs random 48%). Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 6 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051 定论)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 是 classic Goodhart。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **Single-logic risk**: plan006 Prolog validates generality.\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-80%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-80% vs 52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality.\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound.     "originalFile": "# Paper Framing — solver-trace-ablation\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (NLP and Symbolic Reasoning track)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Claims (3 条 — Goodhart 主线诊断/分析论文)\n\n| Claim | Status | 闭环标志 |\n|-------|--------|----------|\n| Claim 1: STA 诊断能力 | ✅ 已闭环 | 3-seed + 1000-step + hacking taxonomy (6 modes) |\n| Claim 2: Goodhart / ISR 无结构优势 | ✅ 已闭环 (D057) | 3-seed ISR + 3-seed random + premise-count-matched + SFT baseline |\n| Claim 3: 跨逻辑泛化 | ⏳ 进行中 | exec-only 3-seed done (D056); STA s42 done (100% H7), s43/s44 RUNNING |\n\n### Claim 1: ISR as Reward Hacking Diagnostic ✅\nSolver Trace Ablation (STA) derives ISR (Intensional Structural Reward) via component necessity testing (leave-one-out ablation on solver inputs). ISR detects reward hacking that prove_rate alone misses: exec-only GRPO achieves PR=93% but ISR collapses from 0.6423 (100 steps) to 0.1324 (500 steps) to 0.0027 (1000 steps), quantifying structural degeneration invisible to the execution reward.\n\n**证据**：\n- plan003: 100-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=32%, ISR=0.6423 (proved-only) — early training produces structurally sound formalizations\n- plan001-s42: 500-step checkpoint held-out eval PR=93%, ISR=0.1324, median_ISR=0.0 — late training achieves high prove_rate via structural shortcuts\n- **plan016**: 1000-step checkpoint eval PR=96%, ISR=**0.0027** — ISR 崩塌持续恶化，无回升。94.8% proved 样本 ISR=0。矛盾爆炸式崩塌（components 6.58→18.55）。延长训练是 reward hacking 随时间恶化的干净消融证据\n- ISR 崩塌轨迹完整链：0.6423 (100步) → 0.1324 (500步) → 0.0027 (1000步)\n- 3-seed consistency: s42 redundant embedding (H1=87.1%, ISR=0.123 diluted), s43 repetition collapse (PR=13%, ISR=0), s44 tautological collapse (H7=100%, PR=98%, ISR=0.57 fake-high, n_comp=2.04). All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\n- Hacking taxonomy: 6 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation)\n\n### Claim 2: ISR as Reward Exhibits Meta-Level Goodhart ✅ (D033→D043→D051→D057 闭环)\nISR 作为 proxy metric 展现 **meta-level Goodhart 效应**：当 ISR 从诊断指标转变为优化目标，模型不仅在高剂量 Goodhart PR（β=0.3 PR 崩塌），还在低剂量 Goodhart ISR 度量本身（β=0.1，2/3 seeds 通过 conclusion duplication 膨胀 ISR）。ISR-as-reward **不提供任何真实结构优势**。\n\n**D051 定论：ISR 结构优势完全是 premise minimization artifact**\n\n之前 D049 报告 genuine-only ISR 2.4x（ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252），但 premise-count-matched 分析证明这完全由 ultra-short proofs 驱动：\n\n| 条件 | n | Mean n_prem | Mean ISR |\n|------|---|-------------|----------|\n| ISR s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 11 | ~5.5 | **0.2578** |\n| Random s42 genuine, n_prem≥3 | 21 | ~6.5 | **0.2434** |\n| **Ratio** | — | — | **1.06×** |\n\n- 控制 premise count 后，ISR vs Random 无差异 (1.06x)\n- ISR s42 的 15/26 \"genuine\" 样本只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 删 1/2 → 50%+ necessary）\n- ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1（H7 分类器 false negative——单 premise 但 normalization 差异导致未匹配 conclusion）\n- **Premise minimization 不是推理质量改善，是 ISR metric 的数学性质**：更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与证明质量无关\n\n**证据（β=0.1 三 seed + random 对照，D043→D051 定论）**：\n\n| Seed | PR | proved_only ISR | H7 (单premise=结论) | H1 (多premise含结论) | H7+H1 | Genuine %
